\section{Next Derivations: the List and Where Each Was Carried Out}\label{sec:next}
% =====================================================================
The chain above is closed from the anchors down. These are the links still to be built,
in the order that would add the most. Each has since been carried out in the section of its topic (map below); its
status follows each item.
\begin{enumerate}
\item The baryon ratio from the reset: the branching of boundary energy between the
radial mode and the winding channel (\S25.7). Four simpler mechanisms have been ruled
out; the calculation needs a charged coupling and its CP-violating part. \emph{Status (\S25.8): the amount follows from the budget and Sakharov's conditions are met without a new
coupling; the reset's realization is identified (\S51.2).} \emph{Release 1.14.0: at the resonance budget the amount agrees with the CMB and
with deuterium burning, $\eta=6.16\times10^{-10}$ (\S25.8).}
\item The Galactic external-field quadrupole of the threshold family, across the window, in the inner solar system, against
the Cassini bound (\S22.7). \emph{Solved (\S49.10, \S46--47): only the Galactic tide remains, within Cassini's bound.}
\item The dynamical selection of the present epoch: whether a principle---for instance
extremal horizon power---places the observer at the maximum-power crossing of
Theorem~\ref{thm:maxpower} (\S26.5). \emph{Status (\S36.6): the coincidence has $p=0.8\%$; no principle derived.} \emph{Release 1.14.0: the present is the resonance (H3, the
opening principle), and the resonance fixes the dark ratio (\S25.11).}
\item The $H_0$ prediction~\eqref{eq:H0pred} against the next distance-ladder, tip of the
red giant branch and BAO results (Appendix~\ref{app:variance}). \emph{Status (\S16.1): $-7.9\sigma$ from the 2026 ladder consensus, $+0.5\sigma$ from DESI Ly$\alpha$.}
\item The map from the $C_3$ period to the acceleration scale, $a_c=c/\tau_1$, from the
field equations of \S41, and the exposure law of \S22.1 from the same equations. \emph{Derived (\S22.10).}
\item The smoothed, projected defect pattern on the sky, for comparison with the
Commander maps (\S39.3). \emph{Projected (\S39.6): the selection rule survives, the ratios fall.}
\item The covariant completion of the gravity law. The field equation of \S22.4 is the
nonrelativistic AQUAL form; a completion must reduce to it with $\mu_n$ in the window, lens by the same
potential that governs dynamics, propagate tensor modes at $c$ (GW170817 \cite{GW170817}),
give PPN $\gamma=\beta=1$ in the solar system, conserve energy--momentum, reproduce the CMB and
matter spectra at linear order, and be free of ghost and gradient instabilities. A covariant
MOND theory meeting these for its own interpolation function exists \cite{SkordisZlosnik2021};
the task is one whose weak-field limit is the threshold family and whose scalar is the defect condensate. \emph{Constructed (\S46.7): one unit timelike vector is the addition.} \emph{Superseded by \S46: the complete equation is general relativity with the condensate as a source.}
\item The fermion and gauge sectors from the action: a tetrad and spin connection for the
generations of \S42, and the gauge dynamics of \S31. \emph{Status (\S41.7): Koide's relation derived from a $C_3$ circulant on the seed cone; electromagnetism shown independent of the defect $U(1)$.}
\item The perturbation-level cosmology of the defect sector, replacing the standard transfer
functions used in \S26.7 and \S39. \emph{Status (\S47.8): reduced to $\Lambda$CDM with the GRUT budget on linear scales.}
\item The reset spectrum of \S45: the coupling $\epsilon u$ and the relaxation rate $\beta$ on
the reset background. \emph{Status (\S45.9): $|\epsilon u|<1.4\times10^3$.} \emph{Derived (\S45): the spectrum follows from the terminal de Sitter phase and the $C_3$ pair convolution; the tilt measures the carrier's mass and the interaction is suppressed by $A_s$.}
\item The fine-structure constant: $K=0.041741(3)$ from the spectrum on $S^1\times S^3$,
with the running of $\alpha$ (\S43A/42C). \emph{Status (\S43A/C, \S43F): the one-loop calculation gives the
thermal weight $1$, so $K=\tfrac1{24}(1+e^{-2\pi})$ and $1/\alpha=137.035\,999\,152$, $1.2\sigma$ from CODATA; the half factors are the electroweak
$\mathbb Z_2$, the deficit $\pi$ (\S43A/C).} \emph{Release 1.14.0: the thermal dressing is the Boltzmann weight of one quantum of the
spin (\S43F).}
\item The full trajectory of $\Theta$ between its endpoints, from the field equations
with matter (\S43B.8). \emph{Derived in closed form (\S43E).}
\item The sign and size of the birefringence angle from the two-C/S handedness (\S39.4). \emph{Status (\S49.12--49.13): in the rotation reading the phase turns at $0.827H_0$ and $\beta=0.312^\circ$,
$+0.6\sigma$ from the measured angle; the frozen-phase bound of \S52.1 belongs to the condensate reading.}
\item The reference mass $M_*$ of the scale duality (\S37), and the curvature radius
$R$ of the spatial $S^3$ (\S4.6). \emph{Status (\S50.7): $R\to\infty$ from the horizon condition; $M_*$ fixed by the reset displacement.}
\item The orientation and scale of the charged-lepton triangle,
$\delta_\ell\simeq\tfrac29$ and $\mu_\ell^2=313.851$ MeV, why pole masses obey the cone,
and where the quark cones sit (\S42). \emph{Status (\S42.7): $\delta_\ell=2/9$ at $0.7\sigma$; the quark cone is $(c,b,t)$.} \emph{Release 1.14.0: the cone is the vacuum of the
axiom's potential, $Q=(\Xi+2)/3\Xi$ (\S42.8).}
\end{enumerate}

\subsection*{Where each derivation now stands}\label{sec:carried}
The list above names fifteen links still to be built. Each has been carried out, in order, and stated as what is
derived or computed, the result, and what remains; a result that fails a test is recorded as failed and kept. Each
now stands in the section of its topic, as the table gives. The computations are reproduced by
\texttt{efe\_quadrupole.py} and \texttt{next\_derivations.py}.

\subsection*{The order of release 1.2.1}
Release 1.2.1 orders the paper so that every result is read in its final form and nothing read early is corrected
later; no result, derivation or number was removed. The rotation that carries the extra gravity is stated first
(Section~\ref{sec:rotation}), before the sections that use its medium; each derivation of the former Section~46
stands in the section of its topic; the derivation list is kept here; and the superseded results and the excluded
mechanism are kept together in Section~\ref{sec:superseded}, each with what replaced it. Every section of the
previous release has the new number below; within a section the subsection numbers are kept, except where listed.

\section{Version history and verification}\label{app:changes}
\subsection*{Release 1.23.2}
Patch release: specifies the local Auxoviscausal strain determinant and its sign-consistent evolution; gives the local Bullet Cluster strain-contrast calculation and data requirements; and adds the covariant matter/radiation/thermodynamic conservation and perturbation closure. No new field, force, or fitted parameter is introduced. A numerical Bullet Cluster map and a full cosmological perturbation integration remain explicit computational tasks.



\subsection*{Release 1.22.1}
Patch release: derives the conditional Auxoviscausal junction rule from the spatial-volume determinant of the existing conformal metric. The divergent physical strain is not matched directly; the finite remainder $\widehat{\mathcal A}_{\rm aux}=\frac13\ln(\hat V/V_*)$ matches when the conformal spatial metric and the identified flow tube are regular across the boundary. In homogeneous FLRW this remainder carries no separate record of the previous aeon’s total $\ln a$. No field or independent transmission coefficient is added.

\subsection*{Release 1.21.0}
Minor release: derives Auxoviscausality as the covariant retained logarithmic proper-volume strain of the existing phase-flow congruence. The volume determinant gives $D\mathcal A_{\rm aux}=\vartheta/3$; integration along a phase-flow worldline gives the causal memory integral; Raychaudhuri and the Einstein source give the local Ricci-load response, including the shear correction. The FLRW limit reduces to $\mathcal A_{\rm aux}=\ln(a/a_0)$ and links its rate to the horizon information density. No new field or coupling is added. The geometric memory law is closed; microscopic yield-threshold plasticity and dissipative entropy production are explicitly not claimed.

\subsection*{Release 1.20.0}
Minor release: Section~\ref{sec:chirp-recoil} derives collision asymmetry from the existing phase and baryon equations; obtains the relaxation drift and mass-conserving transfer flux by differentiating the existing merger map; derives proper-volume recoil from the congruence expansion scalar; relates that scalar to the existing Einstein/Raychaudhuri equations; derives $v_{\rm rec}=-R\dot\Phi/c^2$ from the weak-field spatial metric and retarded phase current; links its domain average to expansion; and derives $H\propto\iota_H$ and $V_H\propto\iota_H^{-3}$ from the horizon definitions. No independent streaming-velocity amplitude or force is introduced. The sign and magnitude of local volume response follow the profile and metric solution.
The version history from the first draft to this version (formerly this appendix), and the verification
scripts and their reproduction of every number (formerly Appendix~G), are kept unabridged in the companion
file \texttt{GRUT\_v1.19.0\_companion.tex}.
